tool_result: def publish(message="E1 live site refresh"):
    if not os.path.isdir(SITE):
        return {"published": False, "reason": "no site/ folder"}
    _ensure_clone()
    _mirror()
    if not _git(["status", "--porcelain"], CLONE).stdout.strip():
        return {"published": False, "reason": "unchanged", "url": PAGES_URL}
    _git(["add", "-A"], CLONE)
    _git(["-c", "user.name=%s" % AUTHOR[0], "-c", "user.email=%s" % AUTHOR[1],
          "commit", "-q", "-m", message + "\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <[redacted]>"],
         CLONE)
    push = _git(["push", "-q", "-u", "origin", "main"], CLONE)
    return {"published": push.returncode == 0, "url": PAGES_URL,
            "error": (push.stderr or "").strip()[:200] if push.returncode else None}


def enable_pages():
    """Turn on GitHub Pages from main/root via the API (idempotent)."""
    r = quiet.run(["gh", "api", "-X", "POST", "repos/[redacted]-[redacted]/e1-live-site/pages",
                        "-f", "source[branch]=main", "-f", "source[path]=/"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    ok = r.returncode == 0 or "already exists" in (r.stderr + r.stdout).lower()
    return {"pages_enabled": ok, "detail": (r.stderr or r.stdout).strip()[:200]}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("publish:", publish("E1 live site — publish"))
    if "--enable" in sys.argv:
        print("pages:", enable_pages())

def _write(name, body):
    os.makedirs(_OUT, exist_ok=True)
    with open(os.path.join(_OUT, name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(body)


# ---------- pages ----------
def esc(x):
    if x is None:
        return "—"
    if isinstance(x, float) and x != x:          # NaN -> em-dash, never the literal 'nan'
def _data(name):
    try:
        return json.load(open(os.path.join(DATA, name), encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}

def _scoreboard():
    """R-ANALYST-SCOREBOARD: the analyst scoreboard page in the v2 style, shelf-only. Server-rendered from
    reports/ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json — honest about what was captured and what is blocked; never a guessed call."""
    try:
        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        d = {}
    sources = d.get("sources") or []
    srows = ""
    for s in sources:
        state = s.get("state") or ""
        cls = "gain" if state == "REACHABLE" else "warn"
        srows += "<tr><td>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td><td class='%s'>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td></tr>" % (
            esc(s.get("source")), esc(s.get("kind")), cls, esc(state), esc(s.get("reason")))
    ranking = d.get("ranking") or []
    rrows = ""
    for r in ranking[:50]:
        rrows += ("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td>"
                  "<td class='n'>%s</td></tr>" % (
                      esc(r.get("analyst")), esc(r.get("stock")), esc(r.get("horizon")), esc(r.get("n")),
                      pct(r.get("accuracy_pct")), pct(r.get("recent_accuracy_pct"))))
    rank_tbl = ("<table><thead><tr><th>Analyst</th><th>Stock</th><th>Days held</th><th>Calls</th>"
                "<th>Right (lifetime)</th><th>Right (90 days)</th></tr></thead><tbody>" + rrows + "</tbody></table>"
                ) if rrows else ("<div class='panel sub'>No calls captured yet — the sources below are robots-gated "
                                 "or paywalled, and no analyst call is ever guessed.</div>")

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '155,216p' scripts/exams_real.py", "description": "Read exams_real.run"}
tool_result: def run(write=True, limit=None):
    cost_rt, measured = calibrated_cost_rt()
    k, k_meas = LB.impact_k()
    cal = LB.spread_calibration()
    cand = evening.active_daily_direction_signals()
    reader = lambda t: ds.read(int(t), "raw")
    reexamined = certified = 0
    certs = []
    probation = []
    if not len(cand):
        return {"note": "no candidates on disk", "reexamined": 0, "certified": 0}
    rows = cand.sort_values("hold_margin", ascending=False)
    if limit:
        rows = rows.head(limit)
    for _, r in rows.iterrows():
        tok = int(r["stock"]); cond = r["condition"]; metric = r["metric"]
        if not isinstance(cond, str):
            continue
        try:
            daily = TF.glue(reader(tok), "daily")
        except Exception:
            continue
        n = len(daily)
        if n < RVOL_WIN + 40:
            continue
        mask = evening._window_mask(daily, metric, cond)
        if mask is None:
            continue
        positions = list(np.flatnonzero(mask))
        direction = r["direction"]
        fam = "cross-metric" if "+" in str(metric) else (str(metric).split("[")[0].strip() or "other")
        reexamined += 1
        best = certify_candidate(tok, positions, daily, direction, fam, cost_rt, k, k_meas, cal)
        if best:
            certified += 1
            best.update({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam})
            certs.append(best)
        else:
            probation.append({"stock": LB._sym(tok), "family": fam,
                              "reason": "no management variant survived real costs + the luck correction"})
    # trades/day of the certified set = sum of fires across the kept variants over the walk-forward span
    total_fires = sum(c["fires"] for c in certs)
    days = _walk_days(cand, reader)
    tpd = round(total_fires / max(1, days), 2)
    out = {
        "note": "STAGING ONLY — the live book and the live exam are unchanged; switching is a separate ruling.",
        "cost": {"calibrated_round_trip_bps": round(cost_rt * 10000, 1), "measured": measured},
        "grid": {"horizons": list(HORIZONS), "stops_atr": list(STOPS), "trail": list(TRAIL),
                 "variants_per_candidate": V, "luck_z": round(_Z_LUCK, 3),
                 "luck_note": "Wilson lower bound at a Bonferroni z across all %d variants tried" % V},
        "reexamined": reexamined, "certified": certified, "probation": len(probation),
        "certified_trades_per_day": tpd, "walk_forward_days": days,
        "certified_patterns": certs, "probation_patterns": probation[:200],
    }
    if write:
        with open(STAGING_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(out, f, indent=1, default=str)
        _write_md(out)
    return out

"""R-ADAPTIVE-SIZING-LIVE: the adaptive sizer — Wilson-LB accuracy × win/loss at ¼-Kelly on CURRENT equity,
bounded by the pattern's drawdown, a total-risk cap and a cluster cap, scaled by regime and drawdown, with
a fail-closed fall back to flat and every fraction printed."""

tool_use Bash {"command": "grep -nE \"^def |^def size|return.*frac|def _|R_PER\" engine1_rebuild/adaptive_sizing.py 2>/dev/null | head -20", "description": "Read adaptive_sizing API"}

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '45,99p' engine1_rebuild/adaptive_sizing.py", "description": "Read the size function"}
tool_result: def size(scorecard, equity, *, worst_daily_dd_rupees=None, open_risk_rupees=0.0,
         cluster_risk_rupees=0.0, cluster_budget_rupees=None, regime_scale=1.0, drawdown_frac=0.0):
    """Rupees to risk on this fire, adaptively, plus every fraction that produced it (all MEASURED).

    Falls back to FLAT (₹1,000) — byte-identical to the reference account — whenever a required measurement
    is missing, so adaptive is never larger than flat on a number it cannot support. Returns a dict."""
    fires = int(scorecard.get("fires", 0) or 0)
    acc_lb = wilson_lb(scorecard.get("win_rate"), fires)
    win_R = float(scorecard.get("win_R", 1.5) or 1.5)
    loss_R = float(scorecard.get("loss_R", 1.0) or 1.0)

    # cold start / thin pattern / no measured book DD → FLAT, and say why (fail-closed to the reference).
    if acc_lb is None or fires < 30 or worst_daily_dd_rupees in (None, 0) or float(worst_daily_dd_rupees) <= 0:
        return {"risk_rupees": R_RUPEES, "risk_fraction": R_RUPEES / equity if equity else None,
                "mode": "flat-fallback", "reason": "cold-start: %s" % (
                    "too few fires (%d<30)" % fires if fires < 30 else
                    "no measured book drawdown" if not worst_daily_dd_rupees else
                    "accuracy not computable"),
                "acc_lb": acc_lb, "f_star": None, "frac_kelly": None}

    f_star = desk.kelly_fraction(acc_lb, win_R, loss_R)           # Wilson-LB accuracy × win/loss ratio
    frac_kelly = desk.KELLY_FRACTION * f_star                     # declared 1/4-Kelly shrinkage
    base = frac_kelly * float(equity)                            # a fraction of CURRENT equity

    dd_scale = max(0.0, 1.0 - min(1.0, float(drawdown_frac)))    # de-risk linearly as the book draws down
    rscale = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(regime_scale)))
    risk = base * dd_scale * rscale

    # per-pattern cap: never risk more than the pattern's OWN measured worst drawdown in ₹
    patt_dd_R = abs(float(scorecard.get("worst_drawdown_R",
                                        desk.expected_worst_drawdown_R(fires, acc_lb, loss_R)) or 0))
    patt_cap = patt_dd_R * R_RUPEES
    # TOTAL-risk cap: all open risk ≤ the book's MEASURED worst daily drawdown.
    total_cap_left = max(0.0, float(worst_daily_dd_rupees) - float(open_risk_rupees))
    # CLUSTER cap: a correlated cluster's aggregate risk ≤ its MEASURED budget. Until a cluster budget is
    # measured (needs the correlation structure) there is no separate cluster bind — it equals the total
    # headroom, so this is never a naked number and never trivially binds at cold start.
    cluster_budget = total_cap_left if cluster_budget_rupees is None else float(cluster_budget_rupees)
    cluster_cap_left = max(0.0, cluster_budget - float(cluster_risk_rupees))

    capped = risk
    binder = "kelly"
    for name, cap in (("pattern-DD", patt_cap), ("total-risk", total_cap_left), ("cluster", cluster_cap_left)):
        if cap >= 0 and cap < capped:
            capped, binder = cap, name
    capped = max(0.0, round(capped, 0))
    return {"risk_rupees": capped, "risk_fraction": (capped / equity) if equity else None,
            "mode": "adaptive", "reason": "bound by %s" % binder,
            "acc_lb": round(acc_lb, 4), "f_star": round(f_star, 4), "frac_kelly": round(frac_kelly, 4),
            "regime_scale": round(rscale, 3), "dd_scale": round(dd_scale, 3),
            "pattern_dd_R": round(patt_dd_R, 2), "total_cap_left": round(total_cap_left, 0),
            "cluster_cap_left": round(cluster_cap_left, 0)}

tool_result: def _universe():
    """F&O names (from the futures store) as the both-exchanges candidate set + the liquid ETFs."""
    import glob
    syms = sorted({os.path.basename(p)[:-len("-I.parquet")].replace("_", "&")
                   for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(DERIV, "future", "*-I.parquet"))})
    keys = []
    for s in syms:
        keys.append("NSE:" + s); keys.append("BSE:" + s)     # same name on both books
    keys += ETFS + ["NSE:NIFTY 50", "NSE:NIFTY BANK"]
    return keys


def _cross_gap(qmap):
    """For each name present on both NSE and BSE, the last-price gap and whether it exceeds a nominal cross-
    exchange round-trip cost. NOTE (stated in the report): same-name NSE vs BSE is TWO different order books,
    so a gap is only a real arb if BOTH sides are executable — this records the gap; executability is the
    open question the recorder exists to answer."""
    rows = []
    ts = _now().isoformat()
    names = {}
    for key, q in qmap.items():
        try:
            exch, sym = key.split(":", 1)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        names.setdefault(sym, {})[exch] = (q or {}).get("last_price")
    for sym, px in names.items():
        if "NSE" in px and "BSE" in px and px["NSE"] and px["BSE"]:
            gap = abs(px["NSE"] - px["BSE"]) / ((px["NSE"] + px["BSE"]) / 2)
            rows.append({"ts": ts, "sym": sym, "nse": px["NSE"], "bse": px["BSE"], "gap_frac": round(gap, 6)})
    return rows

tool_result: # E1 Terminal v8 — the approved design, exact files

These are the approved prototype's page files (owner said "yes" to this exact look and behaviour).
Each *.dc.html is a full HTML page: keep its markup and inline CSS VERBATIM. Only the data changes.

How to port each file to a server-rendered page:
1. Strip the <x-dc>/<helmet> wrapper and the <script type="text/x-dc"> block; keep <head> fonts/styles and everything inside the outer <div>.
2. Every {{name}} placeholder = a value from the desk's records (see mapping below). Every <sc-for list="{{x}}"> block = a repeated row template; <sc-if value="{{y}}"> = render only when true.
3. Sample values in the files (₹2,00,000, DABUR, 937, 69%, etc.) are placeholders — never keep a sample number on the real site.
4. Buttons that switch tabs/filters in the prototype (state in the script block) become real tabs/filters (hash links or small inline JS) — same labels, same positions.
5. Nav on every page: Leaderboard · Engines · Stocks · Patterns · Data · Results · How it works · [Ask the desk]. Deliverables is NOT on this site — it lives at /deliverables/ (its own file Deliverables.dc.html is the layout for that separate site).

Pages:
- Main.dc.html        → Leaderboard (home): desk cards, desk stats row, equity chart, leaderboard table (Expand toggle), latest signals, Engine chat sidebar (the Messages log).
- Engine.dc.html      → Engines: all engines list with stage, pattern funnel grid, per-engine page (stats cards, performance row, patterns with money made, live pattern table, execution checklist, tabs: How it decides / Positions / Trades / Patterns it uses / 210 stocks).
- Stocks.dc.html      → Stocks: ALL watched stocks × ALL readings (35), sideways scroll, filters (All / Signals today / Ready to trade / search). Heading must say "Stocks the desk watches — N" not just "210 stocks".
- Stock.dc.html       → One stock's page: patterns this stock shows, all readings, 30-day story bars, calls on this stock.
- Pattern.dc.html     → Patterns: list (certified/probation/retired), detail: plain words, exact rule, who calls it, rule-in-readings, record, how it earned its place, calls made with it. Add one line at the top explaining: "A pattern is a tested rule. Only the five with 'live paper' are traded."
- Data.dc.html        → Data: every stream, status "Recording data" / "No data today", rows today, last, last 30 days, days until it can be judged, Excel per row; click → recorded today, used by, days recorded, sample rows.
- Results.dc.html     → Results: one line per test, click → its calls, Excel per test.
- Messages.dc.html    → Messages page (also feeds the Engine chat sidebar): filters All/engines/Desk, search by stock.
- How.dc.html         → How it works: six plain cards.
- Ask.dc.html         → Ask the desk.
- Deliverables.dc.html→ layout for the SEPARATE /deliverables/ site (index tests + Quiet Buying), no names.

def _handle_frozen(now, notify_fn=None):
    """3 identical ⏱ signatures = the build is frozen though the pulse continues. Treat as a stall:
    relaunch the re-scan (resume-guard) and summon ONCE (rate-limited), just like a dead heartbeat."""
    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
    if (now - st.get("last_frozen_ts", 0)) / 60.0 < RESCAN_RELAUNCH_COOLDOWN_MIN:
        return
    if not (os.path.exists(gate.CERT) or os.path.exists(RESCAN_DONE)):
        _relaunch_rescan()
    st["last_frozen_ts"] = now
    if (now - st.get("last_frozen_summon_ts", 0)) / 3600.0 >= REPEAT_HOURS:
        (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b [E1] Progress FROZEN — three ⏱ heartbeats in a row with the "
                              "same counter and commit hash. The pulse is alive but the work is not "
                              "advancing; I relaunched the re-scan (resumes from its checkpoint). If it "
                              "stays frozen, double-click START_E1_ALL.bat.")
        st["last_frozen_summon_ts"] = now
    _write_state(st)

tool_result: def poll_telegram(get=None):
    """Read new Telegram updates; act ONLY on KILL/RESUME from the owner's own chat id. Ignores every other
    chat and every other word. Returns the action taken or None. No web trigger — Telegram + owner id only."""
    token, chat_id = [redacted]()
    if not token or not chat_id:
        [redacted] None
    off = _read_offset()
    import urllib.request
    url = "https://api.telegram.org/bot%s/getUpdates?timeout=0&offset=%d" % (token, off + 1)
    try:
        raw = (get or (lambda u: urllib.request.urlopen(u, timeout=8).read().decode("utf-8", "replace")))(url)
        data = json.loads(raw)
    except Exception:
        return None
    action = None
    for upd in data.get("result", []):
        _write_offset(upd.get("update_id", off))
        msg = upd.get("message") or {}
        if str((msg.get("chat") or {}).get("id")) != str(chat_id):
            continue                                   # only the owner's chat
        text = (msg.get("text") or "").strip().upper()
        if text == "KILL":
            kill(); action = "KILL"
        elif text == "RESUME":
            resume(); action = "RESUME"
    return action


def _tg_creds():
    from scripts import notify as N
    return N._creds()


def _read_offset():
    try:
        return int(open(OFFSET, encoding="utf-8").read().strip())
    except Exception:
        return 0


def _write_offset(v):
    try:
        with open(OFFSET, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(str(int(v)))
    except Exception:
        pass

Goal: status.html must show what the build session is doing right now, at most one status cycle old, even while a HALT is in force. Today nothing writes the "current scene" outside the Claude Code window, and tonight's halt froze the site because the blocked session was also the one pushing it.

1. NOW writer. Add a PostToolUse hook (same mechanism as the existing PreToolUse guard) that overwrites E1-DESK\now.txt on every tool call with one line: machine-clock time · current dispatch item (read from the journal's latest item line, not typed) · tool name · the file path or the first few words of the command · the last line of that tool's output. Pass every field through the same scrubber the public transcript uses; never write env values, tokens, or full outputs. The hook must never raise — a failed write must never block a tool.

2. NOW on the page. The status writer (its own deskproc process, not the build session) reads now.txt each cycle and prints a NOW line at the top of status.html with its age ("2 min ago"). If now.txt is older than three status cycles, print it in the warn colour as STALE with the age — stale must never look live. The build room shows the same line.

3. Halt-proof push. Confirm status.html is written AND pushed by the status writer, never by the build session. Prove it without touching the real HALT file: point the guard at a test halt path via config, create that test file, run one status cycle, show the NOW line still updated and pushed, delete the test file. The test must not reach Telegram or any ledger.

4. Beat truth. The BUILD beat's HALTED flag comes from whether E1-DESK\HALT exists on disk at beat time, with the supervisor's last entry as a second line — never from the log alone. Every BUILD beat and LIVE bar line ends with the status.html link, so any pasted line carries it.

5. Telegram "now". token_ping answers the word "now" with the current NOW line plus the status one-liner. Reply-only; it changes nothing.

6. Tests: hook never blocks, scrubber applied, stale marking, halted-flag-from-disk. All existing tests stay green. Commit after each numbered step.

7. Report: paths, commit hashes, one screenshot of status.html showing the NOW line, and the test-halt proof. Call it "built", not "live" — the auditor confirms it in the running loop in the next audit. End with NOTHING NEEDS THE LEAD or LEAD RULING NEEDED: <question>.

tool_result: def calls_and_portfolio(now, mkt):
    led = paper.read_ledger()
    last = led.drop_duplicates("trade_id", keep="last") if len(led) else led
    calls, open_pos, closed = [], [], []
    for _, r in last.iterrows():
        status = str(r.get("status"))
        long = str(r.get("direction")) == "direct"
        entry = _num(r.get("entry_price"))
        res_R = _num(r.get("result_R"))
        mark_R = _num(r.get("mark_R"))
        entry_date = r.get("entry_date") if pd.notna(r.get("entry_date")) else None
        qd = str(r.get("queue_date"))
        try:
            q_label = datetime.fromisoformat(qd).strftime("%a %d %b").replace(" 0", " ")
        except ValueError:
            q_label = qd
        row = {
            "when": f"Filled {entry_date}" if entry is not None and entry_date else f"Signal at the {q_label} close",
            "date": str(entry_date or qd),
            "stock": render.symbol(r.get("stock")), "engine": "E1 — patterns",
            "sentence": str(r.get("pattern") or ""),
            "direction": "Expecting a rise" if long else "Expecting a fall",
            "horizon": "daily",
            "entry": entry, "entry_note": None if entry is not None else f"at the next open ({mkt['next_open_label']})",
            "stop": _num(r.get("stop")), "target": _num(r.get("target")),
            "levels_note": None if entry is not None else "set at the fill — " + EXIT_RULE,
            "status": status,
            "result_R": res_R, "result_rupees": None if res_R is None else round(res_R * paper.PAPER_R_RUPEES),
            "mark_R": mark_R,
        }
        calls.append(row)
        if status in ("open", "filled"):
            open_pos.append(row)
        elif status == "closed":
            closed.append(row)

    st = _live_state(now)
    fires_today = None
    day_R = None
    if st:
        s = st.get("summary") or {}
        fires_today, day_R = s.get("fires"), s.get("R")
        for t in st.get("trades") or []:
            status = t.get("status")
            row = {"when": str(t.get("fill_ts", ""))[11:16], "date": str(t.get("fill_ts", ""))[:10],
                   "stock": t.get("symbol"), "engine": "E1 — patterns",
                   "sentence": t.get("sentence"), "horizon": "intraday",
                   "direction": "Expecting a rise" if t.get("direction") == "direct" else "Expecting a fall",
                   "entry": _num(t.get("entry")), "entry_note": None,
                   "stop": _num(t.get("stop")), "target": _num(t.get("target")), "levels_note": None,
                   "status": status, "result_R": _num(t.get("result_R")),
                   "result_rupees": _num(t.get("result_rupees")), "mark_R": _num(t.get("mark_R"))}
            calls.append(row)
            (open_pos if status == "open" else closed).append(row)

    calls.sort(key=lambda c: (c["date"], c["when"]), reverse=True)
    r = pd.Series([c["result_R"] for c in closed if c["result_R"] is not None], dtype=float)
    wins, losses = r[r > 0].sum(), -r[r < 0].sum()
    curve, run = [], 0.0
    for c in sorted([c for c in closed if c["result_R"] is not None], key=lambda c: (c["date"], c["when"])):
        run += c["result_R"]
        curve.append({"date": c["date"], "R": round(run, 3)})

Pages: Now · Journal · Commits+diffs · Tests · Supervisor · Messages · Reports · Screenshots · Queue · Eyes map. Every page shows its own written time at the top.

2. Messages. Every line the bot sent and every command it received, with times, scrubbed. Source is a local incoming/outgoing log that token_ping writes — add it if it does not exist.

3. Reports. Every builder end-of-dispatch report is written to docs/reports/<date>-<ruling>.md BEFORE it is posted anywhere; every audit and supervisor verdict too. Mirror renders them newest first.

4. Screenshots. Every screenshot any session takes lands in the mirror with time, ruling, and page.

5. Queue. Rulings archived / in progress / pending / done, derived from docs/dispatches and the journal. The beat's "next:" line comes from this, never typed.

6. Ruling inbox. A Telegram message from the owner's chat that starts with "→ BUILD" is archived verbatim to docs/dispatches/inbox/<time>.md and becomes the next queue item; the build session's continue mechanism (R-AUTO-CONTINUE) takes inbox items in order. A message starting with "→ AUDIT" triggers a fresh independent audit run exactly the way the supervisor runs (docs/AUDITOR-TEMPLATE.md, fresh model, reads only repo + mirror files), whose report lands on Reports and as one Telegram line. The interactive audit session is retired. Bot replies "queued: <first words>" so the owner knows it landed. Owner chat id only.

7. Every Telegram line ends with the mirror link.

8. Eyes map. docs/EYES.md, linked from status.html: for every watcher (status page, each build-room page, supervisor, watchdog, dead-man switch, Telegram, audit runs, chat lead) four columns — sees what · how old at most (measured from the writer's cadence, not typed) · what freezes it · what still needs the owner.

        cls = "gain" if _num(rr) >= 0 else "loss"
        crows += ("<tr><td>E1</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n %s'>%s</td>"

            "border-radius:8px'></div>"
